### Task 10: Kavita admin proof, read-only (phase C, first)

Spec sections 4.3 and 13 (risk 3). Phase C reads other people's reading with the admin key. Kavita's endpoints for that are unverified; this task proves them against dev's Kavita, GET only, printing field names and counts, never a key, a token, a name or a number that belongs to someone. Its only commit records the result in the spec.

**Files:**
- Create (scratch, never committed): `$SCRATCH/insights_kavita_probe.py`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-insights-design.md` (section 4.3, a "Proof" paragraph)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces for Task 11: whether the admin token can read another user's totals (`/api/Stats/user-read?userId=`), the users list's id and username fields (`/api/Users`), and whether reading history answers (`/api/Stats/user/reading-history?userId=`) with a chapter id and which date field.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the probe**

```python
"""Insights, Task 10: can the admin key read another Kavita user's reading? Read-only.

  ssh webserver "docker exec -i webservarr-dev python - <mode>" < insights_kavita_probe.py

Modes:
  users     /api/Users with the admin token: status, how many, the fields of one entry
  stats     /api/Stats/user-read for a user who is NOT the key's own account: status and field names
  history   /api/Stats/user/reading-history for that user: status, how many, the fields of one entry

Prints statuses, counts and field names only: never a key, a token, a name, an id or a figure.
GET only, apart from the token exchange the catalog makes (kavita._token).
"""
import asyncio
import sys

import httpx

from app.integrations import kavita


async def main(mode: str) -> None:
    base, key = kavita._config()
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=kavita.TIMEOUT) as client:
        token = await kavita._token(client, base, key)
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}

        async def get(path, **params):
            r = await client.get(f"{base}{path}", headers=headers, params=params)
            try:
                body = r.json()
            except ValueError:
                body = None
            return r.status_code, body

        status, me = await get("/api/Account")
        own = me.get("id") if isinstance(me, dict) else None
        status, users = await get("/api/Users")
        others = [u for u in users if isinstance(u, dict) and u.get("id") != own] if isinstance(users, list) else []
        if mode == "users":
            print("status:", status, "users:", len(users) if isinstance(users, list) else type(users).__name__)
            print("fields:", sorted(users[0]) if isinstance(users, list) and users else None)
            return
        if not others:
            print("no other user to try (only the key's own account exists)")
            return
        other = others[0]["id"]
        if mode == "stats":
            for path in ("/api/Stats/user-read", f"/api/Stats/user/{other}/read"):
                status, body = await get(path, userId=other) if "user-read" in path else await get(path)
                print(path.replace(str(other), "<id>"), "status:", status,
                      "fields:", sorted(body) if isinstance(body, dict) else type(body).__name__)
        elif mode == "history":
            status, body = await get("/api/Stats/user/reading-history", userId=other)
            print("status:", status, "entries:", len(body) if isinstance(body, list) else type(body).__name__)
            print("fields:", sorted(body[0]) if isinstance(body, list) and body and isinstance(body[0], dict) else None)


asyncio.run(main(sys.argv[1]))
```

`ssh webserver "docker exec -i webservarr-dev python - history" < $SCRATCH/insights_kavita_probe.py`

**Decision rule:**
- `users` or `stats` not 200 (401, 403, 404): the admin key cannot read other people's reading. Stop and report to Jordan with the statuses. Tasks 11's sweep is not built; the Phase B snapshots stay the only ebook source (spec risk 3).
- `history` not 200, or no `chapterId`: Task 11 is built without the history half (totals only). Say so in the report.
- If the date field is not `readDate`, Task 11 sets `HISTORY_DATE_FIELD` to the recorded name.

```markdown
<status>, entries carry <chapterId and the date field>. The sweep (Task 11) uses <totals, and history or not>.
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `app/tests/test_insights_kavita.py`:

```python
"""
The nightly Kavita read for Insights (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-insights-design.md,
sections 4.3 and 11): with the admin key, each linked person's totals and the
ebooks their history shows, at most once a night, GET only, against a fake
Kavita on an httpx.MockTransport. A refusal is recorded so the page can say
reading may be a day behind; Kavita not set up is nothing to do.
"""
import asyncio
import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from unittest import mock

try:
    import httpx
    from app.tests import helpers
    HAVE_APP = True
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - the laptop has no app dependencies
    HAVE_APP = False
if HAVE_APP:
    from app.integrations import kavita
    from app.models import Book, EbookPlace, KavitaLink, ReadingTotal
    from app.services import insights_kavita, insights_store

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 3, 0)
ME, THEM = "plex:1001", "plex:2002"


class FakeKavita:
    def __init__(self, stats_status=200):
        self.stats_status = stats_status
        self.calls = []

    def handler(self, request):
        path, params = request.url.path, dict(request.url.params)
        self.calls.append((request.method, path))
        if request.method == "POST" and path == "/api/Plugin/authenticate":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"token": "ADMIN-TOKEN"})
        if request.method != "GET":
            raise AssertionError(f"unexpected write: {request.method} {path}")
        assert request.headers.get("authorization") == "Bearer ADMIN-TOKEN"
        if path == "/api/Users":
            return httpx.Response(200, json=[{"id": 7, "username": "sam"}, {"id": 8, "username": "Kim"}])
        if path == "/api/Stats/user-read":
            pages = {"7": 500, "8": 80}[params["userId"]]
            return httpx.Response(self.stats_status, json={"totalPagesRead": pages, "totalWordsRead": 1,
                                                           "timeSpentReading": 9})
        if path == "/api/Stats/user/reading-history":
            if params["userId"] == "7":
                return httpx.Response(200, json=[{"chapterId": 11, insights_kavita.HISTORY_DATE_FIELD: "2026-10-09T20:00:00"},
                                                 {"chapterId": 999, insights_kavita.HISTORY_DATE_FIELD: "2026-10-09T21:00:00"}])
            return httpx.Response(200, json=[])
        raise AssertionError(f"unexpected Kavita call: {path}")


@unittest.skipUnless(HAVE_APP, "app import needs the container's dependencies")
class Sweep(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.Session = helpers.make_sessionmaker()
        self.db = self.Session()
        self.addCleanup(self.db.close)
        self.db.add_all([
            KavitaLink(identity=ME, kavita_user_id=7, kavita_username="sam", linked_at=NOW),
            KavitaLink(identity=THEM, kavita_user_id=None, kavita_username="kim", linked_at=NOW),
            Book(id=1, title="Dune", sort_title="Dune", author="", series="", description="", kavita_chapter_id=11,
                 kavita_series_id=9, cover_source="kavita", updated_at=NOW)])
        self.db.commit()

    def run_sweep(self, fake, now=NOW, configured=True):
        config = mock.patch.object(kavita, "_config", return_value=("http://kavita.test", "KEY-SENTINEL")) if configured \
            else mock.patch.object(kavita, "_config", side_effect=kavita.KavitaUnavailable("Kavita is not set up"))
        with config, mock.patch.object(insights_kavita, "SessionLocal", self.Session), \
                mock.patch.object(insights_kavita, "_client", lambda: httpx.AsyncClient(
                    transport=httpx.MockTransport(fake.handler), timeout=5)):
            return asyncio.run(insights_kavita.sweep(now))

    def test_totals_and_opened_books_for_everyone_linked(self):
        self.db.add(EbookPlace(identity=ME, book_id=1, page=40, pages=300, read_at=datetime(2026, 10, 1), seen_at=NOW))
        self.db.commit()
        fake = FakeKavita()
        got = self.run_sweep(fake)
        self.assertEqual((got["people"], got["unmatched"]), (2, 1))
        self.db.expire_all()
        self.assertEqual(sorted((t.identity, t.pages) for t in self.db.query(ReadingTotal)), [(ME, 500), (THEM, 80)])
        place = self.db.query(EbookPlace).one()
        self.assertEqual((place.page, place.pages, place.read_at), (40, 300, datetime(2026, 10, 9, 20, 0)))
        self.assertEqual({m for m, _p in fake.calls}, {"POST", "GET"})
        self.assertEqual(insights_kavita.last_error(self.db), "")

    def test_once_a_night(self):
        self.run_sweep(FakeKavita())
        fake = FakeKavita()
        self.assertEqual(self.run_sweep(fake, now=NOW + timedelta(hours=5)), {"skipped": True})
        self.assertEqual(fake.calls, [])
        self.assertNotEqual(self.run_sweep(FakeKavita(), now=NOW + timedelta(hours=21)), {"skipped": True})

    def test_a_refusal_is_recorded_and_tried_again_in_an_hour(self):
        self.run_sweep(FakeKavita(stats_status=403))
        self.db.expire_all()
        self.assertTrue(insights_kavita.last_error(self.db))
        self.assertEqual(self.run_sweep(FakeKavita(), now=NOW + timedelta(minutes=30)), {"skipped": True})
        self.run_sweep(FakeKavita(), now=NOW + timedelta(minutes=61))
        self.db.expire_all()
        self.assertEqual(insights_kavita.last_error(self.db), "")

    def test_kavita_not_set_up_is_nothing_to_do(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_sweep(FakeKavita(), configured=False), {"skipped": True})
        self.assertEqual(insights_kavita.last_error(self.db), "")

    def test_a_failed_sweep_makes_answers_say_kavita(self):
        from app.routers import insights as insights_router
        from app.services import insights
        self.run_sweep(FakeKavita(stats_status=403))
        with mock.patch.object(insights, "plex_people", mock.AsyncMock(return_value=("", {}))), \
                mock.patch.object(insights, "plex_plays", mock.AsyncMock(return_value=[])):
            src = asyncio.run(insights_router._sources(self.db, None))
        self.assertEqual(src.unavailable, ["kavita"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Opened books in the store**

In `app/services/insights_store.py` (add `from sqlalchemy import case` to the imports):

```python
def note_ebook_opened(db: Session, identity: str, book_id: int, read_at: Optional[datetime],
                      now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> None:
    """A book the nightly sweep saw in this person's Kavita history: kept as
    opened (page 0 of 0: how far is not known) unless WebServarr already holds
    their place in it, whose page stays and whose read time only moves on."""
    at = now or now_utc()
    E = EbookPlace
    stmt = sqlite_insert(E).values(identity=identity, book_id=book_id, page=0, pages=0, read_at=read_at, seen_at=at)
    later = case((E.read_at.is_(None), stmt.excluded.read_at),
                 (stmt.excluded.read_at > E.read_at, stmt.excluded.read_at), else_=E.read_at)
    db.execute(stmt.on_conflict_do_update(index_elements=[E.identity, E.book_id],
                                          set_={"read_at": later, "seen_at": at}))
    db.commit()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The sweep**

Create `app/services/insights_kavita.py`:

```python
"""
The nightly Kavita read for Insights (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-insights-design.md,
section 4.3, phase C). With the admin key (the catalog's own exchange,
kavita._token), for everyone who has connected Kavita (kavita_links): their
lifetime totals (reading_totals, so days without a visit fill in) and the
ebooks their reading history shows (ebook_places, as opened books only; a
place WebServarr already holds keeps its page).

Run from the notification poller's leader loop, which asks every
CHECK_INTERVAL; it runs at most once per SWEEP_EVERY, by a settings row, so a
restart or a new leader does not run it twice. One person at a time: Kavita
is one small server across the tunnel. Every call is a GET, after the token
exchange. A failure is recorded (ERROR_KEY: a fixed sentence, never an
address or a key) so the page can say reading may be a day behind, and the
sweep is tried again an hour later; the next sweep that works clears it.
Kavita not set up is nothing to do, not a failure.
"""
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Dict, Optional

import httpx
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.integrations import kavita
from app.models import Book, KavitaLink, Setting
from app.services import insights_store
from app.utils import utc_iso

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

CHECK_INTERVAL = 10 * 60
SWEEP_EVERY = timedelta(hours=20)
RETRY_AFTER = timedelta(hours=1)
SWEPT_AT_KEY = "insights.kavita_swept_at"
ERROR_KEY = "insights.kavita_sweep_error"
# The reading history's date field, as Task 10's proof recorded it.
HISTORY_DATE_FIELD = "readDate"


def _client() -> httpx.AsyncClient:
    """A client per sweep. The tests replace this with one on a MockTransport."""
    return httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=kavita.TIMEOUT)


def _setting(db: Session, key: str) -> str:
    row = db.query(Setting.value).filter(Setting.key == key).first()
    return (row[0] or "") if row else ""


def _put(db: Session, key: str, value: str, description: str) -> None:
    row = db.query(Setting).filter(Setting.key == key).first()
    if row is None:
        db.add(Setting(key=key, value=value, description=description))
    else:
        row.value = value
    db.commit()


def last_error(db: Session) -> str:
    """Why the last sweep failed, or "" when it worked (or none has run)."""
    return _setting(db, ERROR_KEY)


def _due(db: Session, now: datetime) -> bool:
    try:
        last = datetime.fromisoformat(_setting(db, SWEPT_AT_KEY).replace("Z", "+00:00")).replace(tzinfo=None)
    except ValueError:
        return True
    return now - last >= SWEEP_EVERY or last - now > SWEEP_EVERY      # a clock that jumped back


def _when(value) -> Optional[datetime]:
    try:
        found = datetime.fromisoformat(str(value).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return found.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None) if found.tzinfo else found


async def _history(client, base: str, headers: dict, db: Session, identity: str, user_id: int,
                   books: Dict[int, int], now: datetime) -> int:
    """The ebooks this person's reading history shows, kept as opened. Returns
    how many history entries named no catalog book (a chapter that is not a
    book's own: one inside a numbered volume)."""
    entries, _headers = await kavita._get_json(client, "GET", f"{base}/api/Stats/user/reading-history", headers,
                                               params={"userId": user_id})
    unmatched = 0
    for entry in entries if isinstance(entries, list) else []:
        chapter = entry.get("chapterId") if isinstance(entry, dict) else None
        if chapter not in books:
            unmatched += 1
            continue
        insights_store.best_effort(db, "an opened ebook", insights_store.note_ebook_opened, identity, books[chapter],
                                   _when(entry.get(HISTORY_DATE_FIELD)), now)
    return unmatched


async def sweep(now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> dict:
    """One sweep when it is due: {"people", "unmatched"}, or {"skipped": True}."""
    now = now or insights_store.now_utc()
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        if not _due(db, now):
            return {"skipped": True}
        try:
            base, key = kavita._config()
        except kavita.KavitaUnavailable:
            return {"skipped": True}                                  # Kavita is not set up
        links = db.query(KavitaLink.identity, KavitaLink.kavita_user_id, KavitaLink.kavita_username).all()
        if not links:
            return {"skipped": True}
        _put(db, SWEPT_AT_KEY, utc_iso(now), "Insights' Kavita sweep last ran (internal)")
        books = {chapter: book_id for book_id, chapter in db.query(Book.id, Book.kavita_chapter_id)
                 .filter(Book.merged_into.is_(None), Book.kavita_chapter_id.isnot(None))}
        people = unmatched = 0
        try:
            async with _client() as client:
                headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {await kavita._token(client, base, key)}"}
                users, _h = await kavita._get_json(client, "GET", f"{base}/api/Users", headers)
                by_name = {str(u.get("username") or "").casefold(): u.get("id") for u in users
                           if isinstance(u, dict) and isinstance(u.get("id"), int)} if isinstance(users, list) else {}
                for identity, user_id, username in links:
                    user_id = user_id or by_name.get((username or "").casefold())
                    if not isinstance(user_id, int):
                        continue
                    totals, _h = await kavita._get_json(client, "GET", f"{base}/api/Stats/user-read", headers,
                                                        params={"userId": user_id})
                    totals = totals if isinstance(totals, dict) else {}
                    insights_store.best_effort(db, "reading totals", insights_store.record_reading_totals, identity,
                                               {"pages": totals.get("totalPagesRead"),
                                                "words": totals.get("totalWordsRead"),
                                                "hours": totals.get("timeSpentReading")}, now)
                    unmatched += await _history(client, base, headers, db, identity, user_id, books, now)
                    people += 1
        except kavita.KavitaUnavailable as exc:
            logger.warning("Insights' Kavita sweep failed: %s", exc)
            _put(db, ERROR_KEY, str(exc)[:200], "Why Insights' last Kavita sweep failed (internal)")
            _put(db, SWEPT_AT_KEY, utc_iso(now - SWEEP_EVERY + RETRY_AFTER), "Insights' Kavita sweep last ran (internal)")
            return {"people": people, "unmatched": unmatched, "error": True}
        _put(db, ERROR_KEY, "", "Why Insights' last Kavita sweep failed (internal)")
        return {"people": people, "unmatched": unmatched}
    finally:
        db.close()
```

(`kavita.KavitaUnavailable`'s text is always a fixed sentence, never an address or a key: `app/integrations/kavita.py`'s docstring.)

### Task 12: Live sessions: Plexamp listening now, and reading in WebServarr's reader (phase C)

Spec sections 4.5 and 7 (Right now). Plex apps' sessions come from the server's `/status/sessions` Track elements in the audiobook section (a sibling of `get_active_streams`, which stays video only); WebServarr's reader is seen at the Kavita proxy, as it saves progress, and noted in Redis for five minutes. Nothing new is written to Plex or Kavita.

```python
SESSIONS_XML = """<MediaContainer size="4">
  <Track ratingKey="1001" parentRatingKey="100" parentIndex="1" librarySectionID="12" title="Chapter 1"
         parentTitle="Dune" grandparentTitle="Frank Herbert" viewOffset="60000" duration="600000">
    <User id="1" title="Owner"/><Player product="Plexamp" state="playing"/>
  </Track>
  <Track ratingKey="2001" parentRatingKey="200" parentIndex="2" librarySectionID="12" title="Part 2"
         parentTitle="Emma" grandparentTitle="Jane Austen" viewOffset="5" duration="9">
    <User id="4242" title="Sam"/><Player product="WebServarr" state="paused"/>
  </Track>
  <Track ratingKey="3001" parentRatingKey="300" librarySectionID="3" title="A song"><User id="5"/><Player product="Plexamp"/></Track>
  <Video ratingKey="9" librarySectionID="1" title="A film"><User id="5"/></Video>
</MediaContainer>"""


class FakeRedis:
    """get, set with ex, and scan_iter: what the reading note needs."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.data = {}

    async def get(self, key):
        return self.data.get(key)

    async def set(self, key, value, ex=None):
        self.data[key] = value.encode() if isinstance(value, str) else value
        return True

    async def scan_iter(self, match=None, count=None):
        prefix = (match or "*").rstrip("*")
        for key in list(self.data):
            if key.startswith(prefix):
                yield key.encode()
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Plex apps' sessions**

In `app/integrations/plex.py`, after `get_active_streams`:

```python
class PlexSessionsUnavailable(Exception):
    """Plex isn't set up, didn't answer, or sent something unreadable. Fixed text."""


def _whole(value) -> int:
    try:
        return max(0, int(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 0


async def audiobook_sessions(section: str) -> list:
    """What Plex apps are playing in the audiobook library now: the Track
    elements of the server's /status/sessions in library `section`, read with
    the admin token, for the admin's Insights: [{"account", "book_key",
    "title", "album", "author", "offset_ms", "duration_ms", "state",
    "product"}]. `account` is Plex's own (the owner is "1"). The web player's
    own session (product WebServarr) is listed too; the caller leaves it out.
    get_active_streams, the dashboard's video list, is unchanged. Raises
    PlexSessionsUnavailable."""
    config = _get_config()
    if not config["url"] or not config["token"]:
        raise PlexSessionsUnavailable("Plex is not configured")
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=TIMEOUT, verify=False) as client:
            resp = await client.get(f"{config['url']}/status/sessions", headers={"X-Plex-Token": config["token"]})
    except httpx.HTTPError:
        raise PlexSessionsUnavailable("Plex didn't answer") from None
    if resp.status_code != 200:
        raise PlexSessionsUnavailable(f"Plex answered HTTP {resp.status_code}")
    try:
        root = ET.fromstring(resp.text)
    except ET.ParseError:
        raise PlexSessionsUnavailable("Plex sent something unreadable") from None
    found = []
    for track in root.findall("Track"):
        album = track.get("parentRatingKey") or ""
        user = track.find("User")
        player = track.find("Player")
        account = (user.get("id") if user is not None else "") or ""
        if track.get("librarySectionID") != section or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{1,20}", album) or not account:
            continue
        found.append({"account": account, "book_key": f"{album}:{_whole(track.get('parentIndex')) or 1}",
                      "title": track.get("title") or "", "album": track.get("parentTitle") or "",
                      "author": track.get("grandparentTitle") or "",
                      "offset_ms": _whole(track.get("viewOffset")), "duration_ms": _whole(track.get("duration")),
                      "state": (player.get("state") if player is not None else "") or "playing",
                      "product": (player.get("product") if player is not None else "") or ""})
    return found
```

```python
SESSIONS_TTL = 15
READING_TTL = 5 * 60
READING_PREFIX = CACHE_PREFIX + "reading:"
WEB_PLAYER = "WebServarr"                  # plex_player.PRODUCT: the web player's own Plex session
```

After `plex_plays`:

```python
async def plex_sessions(r) -> Optional[list]:
    """Plex apps playing in the audiobook library now (plex.audiobook_sessions),
    cached SESSIONS_TTL. [] with no audiobook library; None while Plex can't be read."""
    from app.integrations import plex
    from app.integrations import plex_player as pp

    if not pp.player_on():
        return []
    found = await cache_get(r, "sessions")
    if found is None:
        try:
            found = await plex.audiobook_sessions(pp._admin()["section"])
        except plex.PlexSessionsUnavailable as exc:
            logger.info("Plex sessions could not be read for Insights: %s", exc)
            return None
        await cache_set(r, "sessions", found, SESSIONS_TTL)
    return found


def _id(value) -> Optional[int]:
    return value if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0 else None


async def note_reading(r, identity: str, body: bytes, now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> None:
    """Note that `identity` just saved their place in WebServarr's reader (the
    reader's progress body: volumeId, chapterId, pageNum), for Right now.
    Only those three numbers and the time are kept, for READING_TTL seconds,
    under the person's opaque key. Anything else is ignored."""
    if r is None or not identity:
        return
    try:
        sent = json.loads(body or b"null")
    except ValueError:
        return
    if not isinstance(sent, dict) or _id(sent.get("chapterId")) is None:
        return
    page = sent.get("pageNum")
    note = {"identity": identity, "volume_id": _id(sent.get("volumeId")), "chapter_id": sent["chapterId"],
            "page": page if isinstance(page, int) and not isinstance(page, bool) and page >= 0 else None,
            "at": utc_iso(now or now_utc())}
    try:
        await r.set(READING_PREFIX + identity_key(identity), json.dumps(note), ex=READING_TTL)
    except RedisError as exc:
        logger.info("Insights could not note reading: %s", type(exc).__name__)


async def reading_now(r) -> list:
    """Everyone noted reading in the last READING_TTL (note_reading)."""
    if r is None:
        return []
    found = []
    try:
        async for key in r.scan_iter(match=READING_PREFIX + "*", count=200):
            raw = await r.get(key.decode() if isinstance(key, bytes) else key)
            try:
                note = json.loads(raw) if raw else None
            except ValueError:
                note = None
            if isinstance(note, dict) and note.get("identity"):
                found.append(note)
    except RedisError as exc:
        logger.info("Insights could not read who is reading: %s", type(exc).__name__)
    return found
```

```python
def now_view(db: Session, names: Dict[str, str], now: datetime, sessions: Optional[list] = (),
             reading: Iterable[dict] = (), owner: str = "") -> dict:
    """Who is listening or reading now. The web player: a place saved in the
    last NOW_PAUSED whose latest log row is not a leave or an end; playing
    when it was saved in the last NOW_PLAYING by a playing event, else
    paused. Plex apps: `sessions` (plex_sessions; None while Plex can't be
    read), less the web player's own. The reader: `reading` (reading_now).
    Plex's account 1 is `owner`'s plex.tv id."""
```

```python
@router.get("/now")

async def now(request: Request, _admin: dict = Depends(require_admin), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Who is listening or reading right now (spec section 7): the web player,
```

```python
async def _note_reading(user: Dict[str, str], body: bytes) -> None:
    """Note a reader save for the admin's Insights (only the book's numbers
    and the time, five minutes, in Redis). Best effort: never fails the save."""
    from redis.exceptions import RedisError
    from app.routers.tickets import account_identity
    from app.services import insights

    try:
        r = await session_manager.get_redis()
    except (RedisError, OSError) as exc:
        logger.info("Reading note skipped: %s", type(exc).__name__)
        return
    await insights.note_reading(r, account_identity(user), body)
```

```js
    if (typeof item.percent === 'number') bits.push(item.percent + '% through');
```

In `app/tests/js/insights_page.mjs`, in the first case, the Plex app check becomes `rr(now[1].textContent).includes('Paused · in a Plex app')` (unchanged: `device` is empty there), and before the final `console.log` add:

- [ ] **Step 2: The keyboard mode**

In `$SCRATCH/insights-live.mjs`, insert this mode just before the line `  } else if (mode === 'member') {`:

```js
  } else if (mode === 'keyboard') {
    const press = async (key, code, vk) => {
      await b.call('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'keyDown', key, code, windowsVirtualKeyCode: vk });
      await b.call('Input.dispatchKeyEvent', { type: 'keyUp', key, code, windowsVirtualKeyCode: vk });
      await sleep(300);
    };
    await b.goto('/insights', { width: 1440 });
    await sleep(2500);
    let reached = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < 80 && !reached; i++) {
      await press('Tab', 'Tab', 9);
      reached = await b.evaluate(`!!(document.activeElement && document.activeElement.hasAttribute('data-ins-person'))`);
    }
    check('Tab reaches a person', reached);
    await press('Enter', 'Enter', 13);
    await sleep(1500);
    check('Enter opens the dialog', await b.evaluate(`document.getElementById('insDetail').open`));
    check('focus is inside the dialog', await b.evaluate(`document.getElementById('insDetail').contains(document.activeElement)`));
    await press('Escape', 'Escape', 27);
    check('Escape closes it', !(await b.evaluate(`document.getElementById('insDetail').open`)));
    check('focus is back on the person', await b.evaluate(`!!(document.activeElement && document.activeElement.hasAttribute('data-ins-person'))`));
    for (const p of ['30d', '1y']) {
      await b.evaluate(`document.querySelector('[data-period="${p}"]').focus()`);
      await press('Enter', 'Enter', 13);
      await sleep(2000);
      check(`Enter on ${p} chooses it`, (await b.evaluate(`document.querySelector('[data-period="${p}"]').getAttribute('aria-pressed')`)) === 'true');
    }
    check('every section is labelled', await b.evaluate(`[...document.querySelectorAll('#wsPage section[data-arrive]')].every((s) => s.getAttribute('aria-labelledby'))`));
```
